# Paragraph 4. Classification of goods

4. (1) The classification of goods specified in Schedule A shall comply with the

General Rules for the Interpretation of the Harmonized System under the International

Convention on the Harmonized Commodity Description and Coding System as specified

in paragraph 4 of the Customs Duties Order 2017 [P.U. (A) 5/2017].

(2) For the purpose of Schedule A—

(a) only goods described in column (3) shown against the headings in

column (1) shall be exempted. Other goods, though might be classified

under the same headings but which are not specified, are not

exempted;

4

(b) where the description of goods in column (3) are shown in general

against the subheadings in column (2), the exemption shall apply to all

such goods as classified within that subheadings.

5

JADUAL A / SCHEDULE A
